Cell["\<\
in this section, we rescale the unitarity cones as discussed in section 8.4 \

 CellChangeTimes->{{3.919269823767088*^9, 3.9192698572972994`*^9},

Cell[BoxData[{
 RowBox[{
  RowBox[{
   RowBox[{"\[CapitalLambda]", "[", 
    RowBox[{"s_", ",", "l_"}], "]"}], "=", 
   SqrtBox[
    SuperscriptBox[
     RowBox[{"(", 
      FractionBox[
       RowBox[{
        SqrtBox["s"], "-", "2"}], 
       RowBox[{
        SqrtBox["s"], "+", "2"}]], ")"}], "l"]]}], ";"}], "\n", 
 RowBox[{
  RowBox[{"\[CapitalLambda]evenl", "=", 
   RowBox[{"Flatten", "[", 
    RowBox[{"Table", "[", 
     RowBox[{
      RowBox[{"\[CapitalLambda]", "[", 
       RowBox[{
        RowBox[{"vx", "[", 
         RowBox[{"[", "i", "]"}], "]"}], ",", 
        RowBox[{"2", 
         RowBox[{"(", 
          RowBox[{"l", "-", "1"}], ")"}]}]}], "]"}], ",", 
      RowBox[{"{", 
       RowBox[{"l", ",", "1", ",", 
        FractionBox["L", "2"]}], "}"}], ",", 
      RowBox[{"{", 
       RowBox[{"i", ",", "1", ",", "M"}], "}"}]}], "]"}], "]"}]}], 
  ";"}], "\[IndentingNewLine]", 
 RowBox[{
  RowBox[{"\[CapitalLambda]oddl", "=", 
   RowBox[{"Flatten", "[", 
    RowBox[{"Table", "[", 
     RowBox[{
      RowBox[{"\[CapitalLambda]", "[", 
       RowBox[{
        RowBox[{"vx", "[", 
         RowBox[{"[", "i", "]"}], "]"}], ",", 
        RowBox[{
         RowBox[{"2", 
          RowBox[{"(", 
           RowBox[{"l", "-", "1"}], ")"}]}], "+", "1"}]}], "]"}], ",", 
      RowBox[{"{", 
       RowBox[{"l", ",", "1", ",",

Cell[BoxData[
 RowBox[{
  RowBox[{"KFF3", "=",
